<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

Now: `saveSessionMeta` stores panel: result.panel (ids). And `metaPanelIds` helper needed. And `contractViews` must support stored contracts with full contract for custom ones.

Design for read side: store in meta.contracts entries {id, version, digest} plus for custom ones maybe the full contract? The doc says contracts are recomputed from roles+budget. For custom ones we can recompute from library (library holds contract). But if the user deletes the interviewer from library later, the session's contract view would break. Better: store the full contract for custom interviewers in meta (small JSON, ~1KB). That preserves history. Let's store `customContracts` in meta? Simpler: store contracts as [{id,version,digest, contract?}] where contract present only for custom ones. Then contractViews uses stored contract when present, else rebuild from presets.

Let me restructure contractViews signature: `contractViews(ids, budget, stored: StoredContract[])` where StoredContract may include `contract?: IntentContract`. Compute: if stored has contract → use it (digest from it); else preset build.

Implement:

```ts
interface StoredContract { id: string; version: number; digest: string; contract?: IntentContract }
```

contractViews:
```ts
export function contractViews(ids, budget, stored = []): ContractView[] {
  const presets = presetContracts(ids.filter(isPresetRole), budget);
  const byId = new Map(presets.map(c => [c.id, c]));
  for (const item of stored) if (item.contract) byId.set(item.id, {...item.contract, budget: budgetView(budget)});
  const out = [];
  for (const id of ids) { const contract = byId.get(id); if (!contract) continue; ... }
}
```

But existing behaviour: unknown role id (e.g., custom id not in stored) simply skipped. Fine.

Wait: contractViews previously took roles and did buildContracts. Callers: app.ts:224 `contractViews(panel, DEFAULT_BUDGET)` where panel is roles. Tests expect `?panel=tech,cto` → 400 unknown. app.ts GET /api/contracts validates roles against ROLES. With custom interviewers, /api/contracts should accept custom ids too. I'll update that endpoint to accept custom ids from library and return contract views with full contracts for them.

Also `assembleSession` uses metaPanelIds and stored contracts; pass stored into contractViews.

Now `metaPanelIds(meta)`: read meta['panel'] as string[]; fallback to stored contracts ids.

Let me write these edits.

</details>

<details><summary>🧠 思考过程</summary>

`presetContracts(ids, budget)` expects BudgetConfig but we only have ContractBudgetView. presetContracts calls buildContracts(ids, budget, 1) which expects BudgetConfig (for the budget view it needs the three fields). Better: change panel.ts's presetContracts and budgetView usage: buildContracts signature takes BudgetConfig? Let me check contract.ts:109 buildContracts(roles, budget: BudgetConfig, version). Now I have a full picture. Let me write the HTTP layer.

Plan for app.ts changes:
1. Import `normalizePanelIds`, `resolvePanel as resolvePanelFromDb`, `presetContracts`, `budgetView`, `PRESET_ROLES` from orchestrator/panel.ts; `optimizeDraft`, `localOutcome`, `bundleOf` from optimize.ts; store functions `listInterviewers`, `saveInterviewer`, `getInterviewer`, `deleteInterviewer`.
2. Remove local `panelOf` / `limitPanel` / `ROLES` / `resolvePanel` local closure; replace with helpers using normalizePanelIds.

Careful: existing tests expect `/api/contracts?panel=tech,cto` → 400 with message containing 'cto' and 'domain_expert'. So keep the unknown-role rejection, but now allow custom ids from DB. Implementation: parse panel list, for each id check `isPresetRole(id)` or exists in DB; unknown → 400 listing available.

Also `GET /api/contracts` default panel: `normalizePanelIds(undefined, panelLimit)` → DEFAULT_PANEL truncated. Tests expect default ['tech','hr','hiring_manager'] — DEFAULT_PANEL is that. Good. And PANEL_SIZE truncation works.

But careful: for /api/contracts with custom ids we need resolvePanel(db, ids, DEFAULT_BUDGET) then viewsOfContracts. Actually `contractViews(ids, budget)` from pipeline handles preset via presetContracts and custom only if stored contract present. For /api/contracts preview of custom ids, we should use resolvePanel(db, ...) then viewsOfContracts. Let me just use resolvePanel for everything in app.ts.

Let me define in app.ts:

```ts
function panelIdsOf(raw: string | undefined): string[] {
  if (raw === undefined) return [];
  return raw.split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(s => s !== '');
}
```

Then for /api/contracts:
```ts
const requested = panelIdsOf(c.req.query('panel'));
const unknown = requested.filter(id => !isPresetRole(id) && getInterviewer(db, id) === null);
if (unknown.length > 0) 400 with available list (PRESET_ROLES + custom names)
const ids = normalizePanelIds(requested, panelLimit);
const roster = resolvePanelFromDb(db, ids, DEFAULT_BUDGET);
return c.json({ panel: roster.ids, budget: DEFAULT_BUDGET, contracts: viewsOfContracts(roster.contracts) });
```

Wait viewsOfContracts isn't exported... it is exported now (`export function viewsOfContracts`). Yes.

For `/run`: 
```ts
const requested = panelIdsOf(c.req.query('panel'));
const ids = requested.length > 0 ? normalizePanelIds(requested, panelLimit) : undefined;
const runInput = { db, sessionId: id, client, model, panelIds: ids, maxClaims };
```
If undefined, pipeline reads from meta.

Hmm but if requested empty, previously `resolvePanel(undefined)` → DEFAULT_PANEL truncated by panelLimit. Pipeline: `wanted = input.panelIds ?? metaPanelIds(session.meta)`; metaPanelIds of a session created without interviewers → [] → fallback DEFAULT_PANEL. But panelLimit wouldn't apply. Test at :423 (PANEL_SIZE=1, no ?panel=) expects only 1 interviewer and 2 probes/2 rounds. So we must apply limit even in default path. So: `const ids = normalizePanelIds(requested.length > 0 ? requested : undefined, panelLimit);` — always pass. But then session meta panel would be overridden by the request default... Actually pipeline uses input.panelIds when provided. If we always provide normalizePanelIds → DEFAULT_PANEL truncated, then a session created with custom interviewers via meta.panel would be overridden by default panel on run. Hmm. But sessions store meta.panel at creation from `interviewers` field. So run should prefer: query panel → session meta panel → default.

Better: compute in app:
```ts
const requested = panelIdsOf(c.req.query('panel'));
const wanted = requested.length > 0 ? requested : metaPanelIds(session.meta);
const ids = normalizePanelIds(wanted, panelLimit);
```
Since metaPanelIds([]) → [] → normalize falls back to DEFAULT_PANEL. And when meta has panel, it's used. Good. Need to import metaPanelIds from pipeline.

Also need validation of unknown ids at run: resolvePanel in pipeline will produce missing → error 500 currently (message includes '已经不在库里了'). Fine but maybe map to 400. The pipeline throws; app catches and returns 500 unless '已经跑过了'. Let's map messages containing '不在库里' to 400.

3. `POST /api/sessions`: accept `interviewers?: string[]`; validate each exists (preset or DB); write meta.panel.

4. New routes:
- `GET /api/interviewers` → `{ presets: ContractView-ish[], custom: [...] }`. Let's return `{ presets: viewsOfContracts(presetContracts(PRESET_ROLES, budgetView(DEFAULT_BUDGET))), custom: InterviewerView[] }` where custom includes id, version, digest, domain, source, contract, systemPrompt, createdAt.
- `POST /api/interviewers/draft` → body { description, jobTitle, jdText, resumeText, before, id, mode?, model?, useAi? } → optimizeDraft or localOutcome. Return outcome.
- `POST /api/interviewers` → body { draft or outcome } → save. Simplify: body { draft: InterviewerDraft, source, domain? , id?, salt? }. Validate draft via interviewerDraftSchema, build bundle, save, return view.

interface InterviewerView {
  id: string;
  version: number;
  digest: string;
  source: string;
  domain: string;
  contract: IntentContract;
  systemPrompt: string;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
}

function interviewerView(row: InterviewerRow, budget: ContractBudgetView): InterviewerView {
  const contract: IntentContract = { ...row.contract, budget };
  return { id: row.id, version: row.version, digest: contractDigest(contract), source: row.source, domain: row.domain, contract, systemPrompt: renderSystemPrompt(contract), createdAt: row.createdAt, updatedAt: row.updatedAt };
}
```

Simplest: add `PERSONA_LABEL` stays Record<InterviewerRole,...>, plus `personaLabelOf(id, contracts?)`. Hmm. In StageRail, panel items are ids; for custom we should show the contract persona name. Let me add helper functions taking an optional name resolver.

Actually simpler: in StageRail and IntakePanel, keep a `Map<string, {name, blurb}>` built from contract views + interviewer library. Let me design:

- `App.tsx` holds `interviewers: InterviewerLibrary | null` (presets + custom), fetched on mount.
- panel state becomes `string[]`.
- `PANEL_ORDER` becomes `string[]` = presets; `nextPanel(current, id)` — needs to preserve custom order too. Rule from comments: normalize order to PANEL_ORDER to keep replay cassettes addressable. But custom interviewers aren't in PANEL_ORDER. Approach: presets first in PANEL_ORDER order, then customs in their own insertion order (stable). That keeps preset ordering deterministic. Custom ids will break cassette addressing anyway (they're not in fixtures), which is fine — custom runs will be live/local or fail with cassette-missing... hmm, that could be a problem for offline demo. But the demo panel is default presets. Fine.

Actually wait: adding a custom interviewer to the panel changes the system prompt for probe stage, so offline replay would fail with cassette-missing → LlmError thrown. Let me check how run handles errors... Probably the session fails with a fatal. That's acceptable UX-wise? For a no-key judge who configures a custom interviewer and hits run in replay mode, the run would fail. Better: when the panel contains custom ids and mode is replay, the UI should suggest switching to live, or we just let the error be explicit. Hmm.

Better idea: The draft endpoint already has local fallback. For *running* with custom interviewers offline, there are no cassettes. I could make the run route fall back? No — silent fallback is against our principles.

Practical approach: in the UI, when panel includes custom interviewers and runMode === 'replay', show a note: 「自定义面试官没有预录卡带，离线回放跑不了；请切到「联网直连」，或在演示时用预设阵容。」 That's honest and simple. Let me do that.

export function nextPanel(current: readonly string[], id: string): string[] {
  const picked = current.includes(id) ? current.filter((item) => item !== id) : [...current, id];

  return [...known, ...rest];
}
```

Let me write it. Keep it manageable but complete.

I need to be careful about TS types: InterviewerDraft, DraftResult, DraftDiff, InterviewerEntry, ContractView.

Also `Persona` name for custom from contract.

Design the component:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Chip, Pane } from './Bits.tsx';
import { draftInterviewer, saveInterviewer, deleteInterviewer } from '../api.ts';
import type { DraftDiff, DraftResult, InterviewerDraft, InterviewerEntry, InterviewerLibrary, LlmMode } from '../api.ts';
import { DRAFT_LIMITS, DRAFT_SEEDS, DOMAIN_HINTS, SENIORITY_LABEL, TONE_LABEL, draftProblem, emptyDraft } from '../studio.ts';
import { personaLabelOf } from '../labels.ts';

export function InterviewerStudio(props: {
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  library: InterviewerLibrary | null;
  runMode: LlmMode;
  runModel: string;
  onChanged: () => void;   // 保存/删除后让上层重取清单
  onError: (message: string) => void;
})
```

State: description, draft, result (DraftResult|null), editingId (string|null), busy, note, error.

When opening, reset? Keep draft across close to avoid losing work. But when `open` flips false→true, keep as-is. Simple.

Actions:
- `handleDraft(useModel)` → calls draftInterviewer({description, before: hasDraft? draft: undefined, id: editingId ?? undefined, useModel, mode: runMode, model: runModel||undefined})
  - If useModel && runMode==='replay' → backend will replay... contract cassette may not exist → falls back to local with reason. Fine.
  - set draft = result.draft, result set, note.
- `handleSave()` → problem check → saveInterviewer({draft, id: editingId ?? undefined, source: result?.source}) → onChanged(), set editingId to returned id, note saved.
- `handleDelete(id)`.
- `handleEditEntry(entry)` → load entry into draft (name/domain/seniority/tone/blurb/goal/tactics from contract) and editingId=entry.id.

For loading from entry: contract has persona{name,seniority,tone,blurb}, goal, tactics; domain from entry.domain.

Also "从例子开始" seeds: click → set description to seed.text.

Render:
- presets section: list with label + blurb, read-only chip「内置」
- custom section: list with edit/delete
- editor: description textarea data-shot="studio-desc", seed chips, two buttons data-shot="studio-ai"/"studio-local"
- result block: source badge, fallbackReason, notes
- fields: name input data-shot="studio-name", domain input list=datalist, seniority select, tone select, blurb input, goal textarea, tactics list with add/remove
- diff table when result.diff.length>0
- system prompt <pre> collapsible data-shot="studio-prompt"
- save button data-shot="studio-save", delete data-shot="studio-delete"

Overlay like HelpPanel with data-shot="studio".

Let me write it. Watch out for string quoting in String.raw: content contains backticks? No. Contains ${? No. Contains double quotes — fine in String.raw with backticks... wait String.raw`...` uses backticks as delimiter, so content must not contain backticks. JSX className strings use single quotes — fine.

Careful: content contains `\u2713`? Avoid. Use plain characters.

Also I must avoid `${` sequences — in JSX template? I won't use template literals in the file; use string concatenation. But JSX attributes with className={'...'} use braces with string concat — fine, no ${.

Actually `String.raw` still interprets `${}` substitution; avoid entirely.
